     January
     一月

          Vanke Service held “Equipment Room Open Day” to practice the concept of comprehensive
          quality and make service for ever better.

          萬科物業舉行「設備房開放日」活動，踐行全面品質理念，讓服務歷久彌新。




     February
     二月

          Xiangying Enterprise Service has obtained the Maturity Level 3 accreditation of Capability
          Maturity Model Integration ( “ CMMI ” ), ISO14001 Environmental Management System
          Certification, ISO45001 Occupational Health and Safety Management System Certification and
          ISO20000 Information Technology Service Management System Certification.

          祥盈企服通過CMMI3級軟件能力成熟度證書、ISO14001環境管理體系、ISO45001職業健康安
          全管理體系、ISO20000信息技術服務管理體系認證。

April
四月

       City Up and the Chinese Society For Urban Studies jointly held the Urban Governance Innovation Practice
       Forum.

       萬物雲城聯合中國城市科學研究會共同舉辦城市治理創新實踐論壇。




June
六月

       Vanke Service completed the construction of 100 “Senior Home” (“ 銀髮之家 ”) senior service
       stations, exploring the construction of senior-friendly communities.

        萬科物業完成100個「銀髮之家」長者服務站打造，探索老年友好型社區的打造。

     September
     九月

          Onewo officially launched “G4 Action” to continuously promote CPR training for customer-facing service
          providers and the deployment of AED equipment in communities, and improve the emergency response
          capabilities of Onewo service providers in various service spaces.

          City Up was selected as the first batch of member units of the Digital Government Construction Service
          Working Committee of Chinese Electronics Standardization Technology Association.

          Cushman & Wakefield Vanke Service obtained ISO41001 Facility Management System Certification.

          Vanrui IntelliTech’s participation in the compilation of the White Paper on Digitally Enabled Industry
          Upgrading (
                    《數字化賦能工業上樓白皮書》                  ) was published.

          萬物雲正式發布「G4行動」，持續推動對面客服務者CPR培訓和社區AED設備配置，提高萬物雲
          服務者在各類服務空間中的應急救援能力。

          萬物雲城入選中國電子工業標準化技術協會數字政府建設服務工作委員會首批會員單位。

          萬物梁行通過ISO41001設施管理體系認證。

          萬睿科技參編《數字化賦能工業上樓白皮書》發布。

October
十月

    Onewo hosted the opening ceremony of the 2023 China International Property Management Industry
    Expo and participated in the exhibition; During the Expo, all cooperative units of Cushman & Wakefield
    Vanke Service jointly held the IFM (Integrated Facility Management) Forum on “Integrating Facility
    Management and Exploring Space Value” and launched the “IFM High-Quality Development” initiative.

    Onewo released the first large-scale model of the property industry “Libra Property Committee
    Workbench” (“天秤業委會工作台”), a property committee service tool that empowers community
    governance with technology and solves the urgent problems and worries of the community.

    The first monograph on the operation and maintenance of super high-rise buildings in China, Guidelines
    for the Management of Facilities and Equipment in Super High-Rise Buildings compiled by the expert
    team of Cushman & Wakefield Vanke Service, was officially published.

    萬物雲承辦2023中國國際物業管理產業博覽會開幕式並參展；物博會期間，萬物梁行各合作單
    位共同舉辦「整合設施管理，探索空間價值」」IFM（Integrated Facility Management綜合設施管
    理）論壇，併發起「IFM高質量發展」倡議。

    萬物雲發布首個物業行業大模型「天秤業委會工作台」，這一業委會服務工具以科技賦能社區治
    理，解決社區急難愁盼。

    萬物梁行專家團隊參編的國內首份關於超高層建築運維領域的專著《超高層建築設施設備管理指
    南》正式發布。

     November
     十一月

          The Chinese and English versions of White Paper on Low-Carbon Properties in Commercial Spaces in
          China jointly compiled by Cushman & Wakefield Vanke Service, RICS and Tongji University were officially
          published.

          萬物梁行聯合RICS、同濟大學三方共同編寫的《中國商企空間低碳物業白皮書》中英文版本正式
          發布。




     December
     十二月

          “G4 Action” of Onewo was launched in Shenzhen, realizing 100% CPR first aid training for all customer-
          facing service providers in Shenzhen and full coverage of AED for residential projects.


          萬物雲「G4行動」點亮深圳，實現深圳市全部面客服務者100%接受CPR急救培訓，住宅項目AED
          全覆蓋。

Letter to Shareholders: Building Momentum, Overcoming                致股東：越過山丘 聚沙成塔
Challenges


The management couldn’t help sighing at the performance              2023年，公司股價表現令管理層唏噓不已。
of the Company’s stock price in 2023. The Company’s share            公司股票回購沒啥效果；提出「中期分紅可
repurchase proved little effect. The proposed scrip dividend         選股」的方案本為增強信心，遺憾被市場負
scheme in relation to profit distribution for the half year of       面解讀為公司缺少現金；因為公司股票四季
2023 was meant to enhance investors’ confidence but was              度交易換手率不足，沒有進入當季恒生指數
unfortunately interpreted negatively by the market as a sign of      篩選 ，股價繼續跌。後來公司發了公開信，
the Company’s lack of cash instead. Onewo failed to be admitted      說明了與恒生指數有限公司溝通的結果，市
to the Hang Seng Composite Index reviewing in the current            場表示認可。
quarter due to insufficient share turnover in the fourth quarter,
which resulted in a continual drop in its stock price. Later the
Company explained this fact by presenting the reply from Hang
Seng Indexes Company Limited in an open letter, which was well
understood by the market.


Secondary market operates as a parallel world to the Company’s       二級市場是與公司經營的平行世界，做空
operations. Although the ups and downs of a company’s stock          的、做波段的都會帶來股價波瀾，但某種意
price can be caused by short-selling and swing trading, they serve   義上，又是公司經營的照妖鏡，惟有穩健真
as a mirror reflecting the operations of the Company to some         實的經營業績以及與股東更公開、充分的交
extent. In this sense, only stable and genuine operating results,    流，才能構成信任，信任與信心是股價的基
along with transparent and ample communication can earn              石。
shareholders’ trust. Trust and confidence form the cornerstone of
stock prices.


In previous years, with high growth and gross margin, many           前些年很多物業公司的高增長與高毛利，着
property service providers had been a good bet in the capital        實在資本市場火了一把，但伴隨着地產開發
market. However, until the sudden contraction of real estate         行業斷崖式退潮，物業公司方知自己在裸泳
development activities in China, these property service providers    並被資本市場狠狠的摔打。失去了信任，就
were not aware that they had been too dependent on property          難再建立信心。
developers and were dumped by the capital market immediately.
Without trust, it is difficult to build confidence again.


Undeniably, real estate owners cannot live without                   不可否認，物業行業是一個不動產持有者的
services from property management industry, so the                   剛需消費服務，這個行業的合同續約率超過
contract renewal rate of this industry is over 85%, which            85%，超過絕大部份SaaS。 2023-2025 這
outperforms most SaaS services. However, the years from              幾年會是行業的分水嶺，此岸是房地產開發
2023 to 2025 will be an up-or-out period for the industry. The       的末游，彼岸是基於不動產持有的消費，而
shift from serving real estate developers who are struggling         此岸已近尾聲，到彼岸的跨越，需要行業中
today and see their industry declining to serving property owners    的優秀公司去用時間證明幾個問題的解題能
who are in high demand for property services requires an A-lister    力。
in the industry to devote time to demonstrating its capability of
solving the following problems.

     Transformation of income structure. It is impossible for major       收入結構轉換。大股東開發商不可能再回到
     shareholders who are also real estate developers to experience       大開發時代。由此帶來的問題是原來高毛利
     another property development boom. The consequent issue is           的開發商增值服務快速回落，因為地產銷售
     that value-added services for developers with high gross margin      緩慢帶來的增量下降，以及開發商應付的空
     slumped due to the shrunken increments resulting from slow real      置房物業費增多。這對財務報表帶來的影響
     estate sales and the mounting property management fees payable       需要時間的消化，但更需要物業公司擺脫路
     by the developers for vacant properties. The resulting impact on     徑依賴、獨立自主轉型的決心。
     the financial statements takes time for property service providers
     to digest and they need to get rid of the path dependency,
     and make up their mind to undergo a transformation and seek
     independence.


     A string of problems brought by growth achieved through              規模增長帶來的系列問題。存量市場的競爭
     economies of scale. The existing market will witness a starker       勢必愈發激烈，「公司規模、品牌」是存量市
     competition for sure. How big a company is and how big its           場選秀中的重要比較因子。然而規模增長的
     name is are two important decisive factors in the rivalry among      背後其實隱藏着一系列問題，比如人力資源
     players in the existing market. But behind growth achieved           與服務品質保障問題。自媒體時代，每一次
     by leveraging economies of scale are a train of problems. For        服務投訴都可能是品牌的滅頂之災，盲目增
     example, there may be defects in human resources and service         長會形成惡性循環。再比如，當公司規模足
     quality guarantees. In the era of we-media, every complaint          夠大時，若因為服務品質下降帶來客戶繳費
     about services can potentially be a brand disaster, and flying       意願降低，同時員工工資又是剛性的支出，
     blind on growth will end up a vicious spiral. For another example,   由此甚至會帶來現金流保障問題。唯有積極
     to a company employing a large number of service personnel,          擁抱技術，並堅持客戶至上的文化才能破解
     if customers are getting unwilling to pay due to a decline in its    規模增長帶來的或有問題。
     service quality while the employee payroll must be paid, this will
     even cause a lack of cash flow. The contingent problems brought
     by growth achieved through economies of scale can only be
     solved when technology is proactively embraced and the culture
     of “customer first” is firmly hold on to.


     We would like to travel with more outstanding peers and climb        期待更多優秀同行，一起越過房地產開發時
     over hills of difficulty faced by real estate developers together.   代的山丘。「喋喋不休，時不我予的哀愁，
     “Can’t stop complaining that we are in bad times, and won’t          還未如願見着不朽，莫把自己搞丟」。
     forget why we start until we make it”.

CONFIRMATION FROM OUR INDEPENDENT                                       獨立非執行董事確認
NON-EXECUTIVE DIRECTORS

2.   Kilo Plan                                                      2.   千人計劃

     The Group actively supports front line workers to pursue            本集團積極支持一線奮鬥者學習深
     advanced study, and subsidizes front line employees to              造，以提供獎學金的方式資助一線員
     achieve “senior high school to junior college transfer (高           工「 高 升 專 」和「 專 升 本 」， 幫 助 他 們
     升專)” and “junior college to bachelor’s degree transfer              實現學歷提升。本集團於2010 年啟
     (專升本)” by providing scholarships, helping them improve              動「雙百計劃」，計劃每年向200 名基
     their academic qualifications. In 2010, the Group launched          層員工開放「高升專」學歷提升名額，
     the “Double Hundred Plan (雙百計劃)”, and planned to                    2021 年，本集團將「雙百計劃」升級
     provide 200 front line employees with the opportunity               為「千人計劃」，每年計劃向 1,000 名
     to improve their academic qualifications through “senior            一線員工開放「高升專」和「專升本」學
     high school to junior college transfer” every year. In 2021,        歷提升名額，為每名參與者提供人民
     the Group had upgraded the “Double Hundred Plan” to                 幣5,000至7,000元的助學金。
     the “Kilo Plan”. The Group plans to provide 1,000 front
     line employees with the opportunity to improve their
     academic qualifications through “senior high school to
     junior college transfer” and “junior college to bachelor’s

DIVIDEND POLICY                                                     股息政策
The declaration and payment of dividends shall be determined        宣派及派付股息將由董事會全權酌情釐定。
at the sole discretion of the Board. The Board shall also take      於考慮是否建議派付股息及釐定股息金額
into account the following factors when considering whether         時，董事會亦應考慮下列因素：我們的經營
to propose dividends and determining the dividend amount:           業績、現金流量、財務狀況、附屬公司向我
our results of operations, cash flows, financial condition,         們派付的現金股息、業務前景、有關我們宣
payments by our subsidiaries of cash dividends to us, business      派及派付股息的法定、監管及合約限制、總
prospects, statutory, regulatory and contractual restrictions on    體經濟狀況、本集團業務的業務周期及可能
our declaration and payment of dividends, general economic          對本集團業務或財務表現及狀況產生影響的
conditions, business cycle of the Group’s business and other        其他內部或外部因素，以及董事會可能認為
internal or external factors that may have an impact on the         重要的其他因素。股息僅可自我們可供合法
business or financial performance and position of the Group, as     分派的利潤中宣派或派付。
well as other factors that our Board may consider important. No
dividend will be declared or payable except out of our profits
lawfully available for distribution.

CORPORATE GOVERNANCE                                                   企業管治
The Group is committed to implementing high standards                  本集團致力於實現高標準企業管治，以保障
of corporate governance to safeguard the interests of the              本公司股東權益及提高企業價值與責任承
Shareholders of the Company and enhance the corporate value            擔。
as well as the responsibility commitments.


The Group is fully aware of the importance of the Board of             本集團深刻認識到董事會對於本集團業務的
Directors in providing targeted guidance for the Group’s business      精準引領以及確保運營透明度和問責機制的
and ensuring the operational transparency and accountability           重要性。董事會深知良好的企業管治是引領
system. The Board of Directors fully understands that good             本集團走向成功、提升股東價值的關鍵所
corporate governance is key to leading the Group to success and        在。因此，董事會精心制定了一系列企業管
enhancing Shareholders’ value. Therefore, the Board of Directors       治政策及制度，旨在全面保障股東權益，並
has elaborated a series of corporate governance policies and           不斷提升企業價值。同時，所有董事秉持公
systems with the aim of fully protecting Shareholders’ interests       正無私的原則，以身作則，積極傳播並踐行
and continuously enhancing corporate value. At the same time,          企業文化，共同推動本集團的穩健發展。
all Directors shall adhere to the principle of impartiality, lead by
example and actively promote and practice the corporate culture
to jointly facilitate the steady development of the Group.


The Company has adopted the CG Code as its own code of                 本公司已採納《企業管治守則》作為其本身
corporate governance, and to the knowledge of the Directors,           的企業管治準則，且根據董事所知，於截至
the Company has complied with all applicable code provisions           2023 年12 月31 日止年度及截至最後實際可
set out in the CG Code throughout the year ended December              行日期，除下述披露外，本公司已遵守《企
31, 2023 and as of the Latest Practicable Date, save as disclosed      業管治守則》所有適用守則條文。
below.


Pursuant to code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code, the roles of          根據《企業管治守則》守則條文第C.2.1 條，
chairman and chief executive should be separate and should not         主席與行政總裁的角色應有區分，並不應由
be performed by the same individual. Mr. Zhu Baoquan is the            一人同時兼任。朱保全先生為我們的董事長
chairman of our Board and the general manager of our Company           兼本公司總經理，且朱保全先生的職務並未
and the roles of Mr. Zhu Baoquan have not been separate as             根據《企業管治守則》守則條文第C.2.1 條的
required under code provision C.2.1 of the CG Code.                    規定區分。

      In view of the fact that Mr. Zhu Baoquan has been managing           鑒於自2011年2月起朱保全先生一直作為董
      and operating the Company as the Chairman, an executive              事長、執行董事兼本公司總經理管理及運營

The Company regards the board diversity policy as a key      本公司將董事會的多元化政策視為推

maintain diversity in our Board. Pursuant to this policy,    我們在甄選董事會候選人時，會綜

candidates’ advantages and the value that they can bring     主要考量標準。通過這一系列的舉
to our Board will be our primary consideration. We are       措，我們致力於構建一個多元化、高

      and for such internal control as the directors determine is          不存在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳
      necessary to enable the preparation of consolidated financial        述所需的內部控制負責。
      statements that are free from material misstatement, whether
      due to fraud or error.

      In preparing the consolidated financial statements, the directors    在編製合併財務報表時， 貴公司董事負責
      of the Company are responsible for assessing the Group’s ability     評估 貴集團持續經營的能力，並在適用情
      to continue as a going concern, disclosing, as applicable, matters   況下披露與持續經營有關的事項，以及使用
      related to going concern and using the going concern basis of        持續經營為會計基礎，除非 貴公司董事有
      accounting unless the directors of the Company either intend to      意將 貴集團清盤或停止經營，或別無其他
      liquidate the Group or to cease operations or have no realistic      實際的替代方案。
      alternative but to do so.

      Group’s financial reporting process.

STATEMENTS

Our objectives are to obtain reasonable assurance about whether        我們的目標是對合併財務報表整體是否不存
the consolidated financial statements as a whole are free from         在由於欺詐或錯誤而導致的重大錯誤陳述取
material misstatement, whether due to fraud or error, and to           得合理保證，並出具包括我們意見在內的核
issue an auditor’s report that includes our opinion. Our report is     數師報告。我們的報告僅向 閣下（作為整
made solely to you, as a body, and for no other purpose. We do         體）作出，除此之外別無其他用途。我們不
not assume responsibility towards or accept liability to any other     會就本報告的內容向任何其他人士負上或承
person for the contents of this report.                                擔任何責任。

Reasonable assurance is a high level of assurance, but is not a        合理保證是高水平的保證，但不能保證按照
guarantee that an audit conducted in accordance with HKSAs             《香港審計準則》進行的審計總能發現存在某
will always detect a material misstatement when it exists.             一重大錯誤陳述。錯誤陳述可以由欺詐或錯
Misstatements can arise from fraud or error and are considered         誤引起，如果合理預期其單獨或匯總起來可
material if, individually or in the aggregate, they could reasonably   能影響合併財務報表使用者依賴合併財務報
be expected to influence the economic decisions of users taken         表所作出的經濟決定，則有關的錯誤陳述可
on the basis of these consolidated financial statements.               被視作重大。

As part of an audit in accordance with HKSAs, we exercise              在根據《香港審計準則》進行審計的過程中，
professional judgement and maintain professional scepticism            我們運用了專業判斷，保持了專業懷疑態
throughout the audit. We also:                                         度。我們亦：

•      Identify and assess the risks of material misstatement          •      識別和評估由於欺詐或錯誤而導致合
       of the consolidated financial statements, whether due                  併財務報表存在重大錯誤陳述的風
       to fraud or error, design and perform audit procedures                 險，設計及執行審計程序以應對這些
       responsive to those risks, and obtain audit evidence that              風險，以及獲取充足和適當的審計憑
       is sufficient and appropriate to provide a basis for our               證，作為我們意見的基礎。由於欺詐
       opinion. The risk of not detecting a material misstatement             可能涉及串謀、偽造、蓄意遺漏、虛
       resulting from fraud is higher than for one resulting from             假陳述，或凌駕於內部控制之上，因
       error, as fraud may involve collusion, forgery, intentional            此未能發現因欺詐而導致的重大錯誤
       omissions, misrepresentations, or the override of internal             陳述的風險高於未能發現因錯誤而導
       control.                                                               致的重大錯誤陳述的風險。

•      Obtain an understanding of internal control relevant to         •      了解與審計相關的內部控制，以設計
       the audit in order to design audit procedures that are                 適當的審計程序，但目的並非對 貴

1.   CORPORATE AND GROUP                                        1.   公司及集團資料
     INFORMATION

     Onewo Inc. (the “Company”) was incorporated in the               萬物雲空間科技服務股份有限公司
     People’s Republic of China (“PRC”) on 20 February 2001          （「本公司」）於2001年2月20日在中華
     as a limited liability company. On 20 March 2018, the            人民共和國（「中國」）註冊成立為一家
     Company was converted into a joint stock company with            有限公司。於2018年3月20日，本公
     limited liability under the Company Law of the PRC.              司根據《中華人民共和國公司法》改制
                                                                      為股份有限公司。

     During the year, the Group was involved in the following        年內，本集團從事以下主營業務：
     principal activities:


     •      Community space living consumption services              •   社區空間居住消費服務
     •      Commercial and urban space integrated services           •   商企和城市空間綜合服務
     •      AIoT and BPaaS solution services                         •   AIoT及BPaaS解決方案服務

     In the opinion of the directors, the ultimate holding           董事認為，本公司的最終控股公司
     company of the Company is China Vanke Co., Ltd. (the            為 萬 科 企 業 股 份 有 限 公 司（「 萬 科 企
     “China Vanke”), a public company established in the PRC         業」，一家於中國成立的上市公司，其
     with its shares listed on Shenzhen Stock Exchange and           股份在深圳證券交易所和香港聯合交
     The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock             易所有限公司「聯交所」上市）。
     Exchange”).

              2.4 Material accounting policies                                   2.4 重要會計政策

                     Investments in associates and joint ventures                  於聯營公司及合營企業之投資

                     An associate is an entity in which the Group has a            聯營公司是指本集團於其擁有
                     long-term interest of generally not less than 20%             長期權益（通常不低於股權表決
                     of the equity voting rights and over which it has             權 的 2 0 % ）， 並 對 其 有 重 大 影
                     significant influence. Significant influence is the           響的主體。重大影響力指有權
                     power to participate in the financial and operating           參與投資對象的財務及營運決
                     policy decisions of the investee, but is not control          策，但對該等決策並無控制權
                     or joint control over those policies.                         或共同控制權。

                     A joint venture is a type of joint arrangement                合營企業是指共同控制一項安
                     whereby the parties that have joint control of the            排的參與方對該合營企業的淨
                     arrangement have rights to the net assets of the              資產享有權利的合營安排。共
                     joint venture. Joint control is the contractually             同控制是指按合同約定分享對
                     agreed sharing of control of an arrangement,                  一項安排的控制權，並且在相
                     which exists only when decisions about the relevant           關活動的決策中要求分享控制
                     activities require the unanimous consent of the               權的參與方一致同意時才存在。
                     parties sharing control.


                     The Group’s investments in associates and joint               本集團於聯營公司及合營企業
                     ventures are stated in the consolidated statement             之投資在合併財務狀況表中以
                     of financial position at the Group’s share of net             權益會計法按本集團應佔資產
                     assets under the equity method of accounting, less            淨值扣除任何減值虧損後列示。
                     any impairment losses.

     2.4 Material accounting policies (continued)                          2.4 重要會計政策（續）
          Fair value measurement                                             公允價值計量

          The Group measures its equity investments                          本集團於各報告期末按公允價
          designated at fair value through other                             值計量其指定為以公允價值計
          comprehensive income, financial assets at fair                     量且其變動計入其他全面收益
          value through profit or loss, financial liability at fair          的股權投資及以公允價值計量
          value through profit or loss and derivative financial              且其變動計入當期損益的金融
          instruments at fair value at the end of each                       資產、以公允價值計量且其變
          reporting period. Fair value is the price that would               動計入當期損益的金融負債及
          be received to sell an asset or paid to transfer a                 衍生金融工具。公允價值是指
          liability in an orderly transaction between market                 市場參與者在計量日的有序交
          participants at the measurement date. The fair                     易中，出售資產所收取或轉讓
          value measurement is based on the presumption                      負債所支付的價格。公允價值
          that the transaction to sell the asset or transfer                 計量乃基於假設出售資產或轉
          the liability takes place either in the principal                  讓負債的交易於資產或負債的
          market for the asset or liability, or in the absence               主要市場或（在無主要市場情況
          of a principal market, in the most advantageous                    下）於資產或負債的最有利市場
          market for the asset or liability. The principal or the            進行。主要或最有利市場應當
          most advantageous market must be accessible by                     是本集團能夠進入的市場。資
          the Group. The fair value of an asset or a liability               產或負債的公允價值乃基於市
          is measured using the assumptions that market                      場參與者為資產或負債定價時
          participants would use when pricing the asset or                   所用之假設計量（假設市場參與
          liability, assuming that market participants act in                者依照其最佳經濟利益行事）。
          their economic best interest.

          A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset                  非金融資產的公允價值計量須
          takes into account a market participant’s ability to               計及市場參與者通過使用該資
          generate economic benefits by using the asset in                   產的最高及最佳用途，或將該
          its highest and best use or by selling it to another               資產出售予將使用其最高及最
          market participant that would use the asset in its                 佳用途的另一市場參與者而產
          highest and best use.                                              生經濟利益的能力。

          The Group uses valuation techniques that are                       本集團採用在當前情況下適用
          appropriate in the circumstances and for which                     並且有足夠可利用數據支持的
          sufficient data are available to measure fair value,               估值技術去計量公允價值，盡
          maximising the use of relevant observable inputs                   量使用相關可觀察輸入數據及
          and minimising the use of unobservable inputs.                     盡量減少使用不可觀察輸入數
                                                                             據。

          All assets and liabilities for which fair value is                 在財務報表內計量或披露公允
          measured or disclosed in the financial statements                  價值的所有資產及負債，均根
          are categorised within the fair value hierarchy,                   據對公允價值計量整體而言具
          described as follows, based on the lowest                          有重要意義的最低層級輸入數
          level input that is significant to the fair value                  據在下述公允價值層級內進行
          measurement as a whole:                                            分類：

                     losses. The cost of an item of property, plant and             物業、廠房及設備項目的成本

                     directly attributable costs of bringing the asset to           致運作狀況及地點以作擬定用
                     its working condition and location for its intended            途而直接應計的成本。
                     use.


                     Expenditure incurred after items of property, plant            物業、廠房及設備項目投入運
                     and equipment have been put into operation, such               作後產生的開支，例如維修
                     as repairs and maintenance, is normally charged                保養開支，一般於產生期間自
                     to the statement of profit or loss in the period               損益表中扣除。在符合確認標
                     in which it is incurred. In situations where the               準的情況下，主要檢查開支於
                     recognition criteria are satisfied, the expenditure            資產賬面值中資本化為重置成
                     for a major inspection is capitalised in the carrying          本。如果物業、廠房及設備的
                     amount of the asset as a replacement. Where                    主要部分須分期替換，本集團
                     significant parts of property, plant and equipment             會確認該等部分為有特定使用
                     are required to be replaced at intervals, the                  年期的個別資產並據此將其折
                     Group recognises such parts as individual assets               舊。

          General approach                                                一般方法

          ECLs are recognised in two stages. For credit                   預期信貸虧損於兩個階段確
          exposures for which there has not been a                        認。就自初始確認以來信貸風
          significant increase in credit risk since initial               險並無顯著增加的信貸風險項
          recognition, ECLs are provided for credit losses                目而言，預期信貸虧損為就未
          that result from default events that are possible               來 12 個月內可能發生的違約事
          within the next 12 months (a 12-month ECL). For                 件產生的信貸虧損計提撥備（12
          those credit exposures for which there has been                 個月預期信貸虧損）。就自初始
          a significant increase in credit risk since initial             確認以來信貸風險顯著增加的
          recognition, a loss allowance is required for credit            信貸風險項目而言，須於風險
          losses expected over the remaining life of the                  剩餘年期內就預期信貸虧損計
          exposure, irrespective of the timing of the default (a          提虧損撥備，不論違約於何時
          lifetime ECL).                                                  發生（存續期預期信貸虧損）。

          At each reporting date, the Group assesses                      於各報告日期，本集團會評估
          whether the credit risk on a financial instrument               金融工具的信貸風險自初始確
          has increased significantly since initial recognition.          認以來是否顯著增加。進行評
          When making the assessment, the Group compares                  估時，本集團比較金融工具於
          the risk of a default occurring on the financial                報告日期出現違約之風險與該
          instrument as at the reporting date with the risk               金融工具於初始確認日期出現
          of a default occurring on the financial instrument              違約之風險，並考慮合理及有
          as at the date of initial recognition and considers             理據且毋須花費不必要成本或
          reasonable and supportable information that is                  精力即可獲得之資料，包括過
          available without undue cost or effort, including               往及前瞻性資料。本集團認
          historical and forward-looking information. The                 為，當合約付款逾期逾30 天
          Group considers that there has been a significant               時，信貸風險會顯著增加。
          increase in credit risk when contractual payments
          are more than 30 days past due.

                     General approach (Continued)                                     一般方法（續）

                     The Group considers a financial asset in default                 如果合約付款逾期 90 天，本集
                     when contractual payments are 90 days past due.                  團視金融資產為違約。然而，
                     However, in certain cases, the Group may also                    在若干情況下，本集團亦可能

          Contract costs                                                  合約成本

          Other than the costs which are capitalised as                   除已資本化為存貨、物業、廠
          inventories, property, plant and equipment and                  房及設備以及無形資產的成本
          intangible assets, costs incurred to fulfil a contract          外，為履行客戶合約而產生的
          with a customer are capitalised as an asset if all of           成本於符合以下所有標準的情
          the following criteria are met:                                 況下資本化為一項資產：

          (a)    The costs relate directly to a contract or to            (a)      成本與主體可以具體確定
                 an anticipated contract that the entity can                       的合約或預期合約直接相
                 specifically identify.                                            關。

          (b)    The costs generate or enhance resources                  (b)      成本產生或增強主體未來
                 of the entity that will be used in satisfying                     用 以 履 行（ 或 繼 續 履 行 ）
                 (or in continuing to satisfy) performance                         履約責任的資源。
                 obligations in the future.


          (c)    The costs are expected to be recovered.                  (c)      成本預期將被收回。

          The capitalised contract costs are amortised and                資本化合約成本按與向客戶轉
          charged to the statement of profit or loss on a                 移與資產有關的商品或服務相
          systematic basis that is consistent with the transfer           一致的系統化基準進行攤銷並
          to the customer of the goods or services to which               自損益表扣除。其他合約成本

              unit exceeds its recoverable amount, which is the higher of            用 價 值 兩 者 中 的 較 高 者 ）， 則 存 在 減
              its fair value less costs of disposal and its value-in-use. The        值。公允價值減出售成本的計算乃基
              calculation of the fair value less costs of disposal is based          於類似資產於具有約束力的銷售交易
              on available data from binding sales transactions of similar           中的可用數據或可觀察的市場價格，
              assets or observable market prices less incremental costs              再減去出售資產的增量成本。當計算
              for disposing of the asset. When value-in-use calculations             使用價值時，管理層必須估計來自資
              are undertaken, management must estimate the expected                  產或現金產生單位的預期未來現金流
              future cash flows from the asset or cash-generating unit               量，並選擇合適的貼現率以計算該等
              and choose a suitable discount rate in order to calculate              現金流量的現值。
              the present value of those cash flows.


              Leases — Estimating the incremental                                    租賃 － 估計增量借款利率
              borrowing rate

              The Group cannot readily determine the interest rate                   本集團無法隨時釐定租賃中隱含的利
              implicit in a lease, and therefore, it uses an incremental             率，因此，其使用增量借款利率計量
              borrowing rate (“IBR”) to measure lease liabilities. The IBR           租賃負債。增量借款利率為本集團於
              is the rate of interest that the Group would have to pay to            類似經濟環境中為取得與使用權資
              borrow over a similar term, and with a similar security, the           產價值相近的資產，而以類似抵押品
              funds necessary to obtain an asset of a similar value to               於類似期間借入所需資金應支付的利
              the right-of-use asset in a similar economic environment.              率。因此，增量借款利率反映了本集
              The IBR therefore reflects what the Group “would have                  團「應支付」的利率，當無可觀察的利
              to pay”, which requires estimation when no observable                  率時（例如就未訂立融資交易的附屬
              rates are available (such as for subsidiaries that do not              公司而言）或當須對利率進行調整以
              enter into financing transactions) or when it needs to be              反映租賃的條款及條件時，則須作出
              adjusted to reflect the terms and conditions of the lease.             估計。

    approved by management covering a five-year period.            前現金流量預測。超過五年期的現金
    Cash flows beyond the five-year period are extrapolated        流量使用以下估計長期增長率進行推
    using the estimated long-term growth rates below. The          算。該增長率未超過現金產生單位經
    growth rates do not exceed the long-term average growth        營所在相關行業的長期平均增長率。
    rate for the related industry in which the CGUs operate.       以下稅前貼現率反映了與相關行業及
    The pre-tax discount rates below reflect specific risks        現金產生單位本身相關的特定風險以
    relating to the relevant industry and the CGUs themselves      及相關地區的宏觀環境。
    and macro-environment of the relevant region.

                 only if, there is objective evidence of impairment           生的一個或多個事件存在減值
                 as a result of one or more events that occurred              的客觀證據，而虧損事件會對
                 post-acquisition and that loss event (or events) has         可靠估計該投資之估計未來現
                 an impact on the estimated future cash flows from            金流量產生影響，則會發生減
                 the investment that can be reliably estimated. The           值虧損。本集團評估，於報告
                 Group assessed that there is no objective evidence           期末，並不存在投資減值的客
                 that the investment is impaired at the end of the            觀證據。
                 reporting period.

          of future economic conditions.

                  The capital reserve of the Group represents 1) the               本集團的資本儲備是指：1)已
                  difference between the par value of the shares                   發行股份面值與收到的對價
                  issued and the consideration received; 2) the excess             之間的差額；2)對價超過已收
                  of the consideration over the carrying amount of                 購 ╱ 出售的非控股權益的賬面
                  the non-controlling interests acquired/disposed;                 值部分；及 3）已授出的以股份
                  and 3) the fair value of the share-base payment                  為基礎的付款的公允價值。
                  granted.


          (ii)    Fair value reserve of financial assets at fair value     (ii)    以公允價值計量且其變動計入
                  through other comprehensive income                               其他全面收益的金融資產的公
                                                                                   允價值儲備

                  value through other comprehensive income                         其他全面收益的金融資產的公

                  equity instruments designated at fair value through              價值計量且其變動計入其他全
                  other comprehensive income.                                      面收益的權益工具產生的所有
                                                                                   重估變動。

35. FAIR VALUE AND FAIR VALUE                                    35. 金融工具的公允價值及公
   HIERARCHY OF FINANCIAL                                            允價值層級（續）
   INSTRUMENTS (continued)

   The Group’s finance department headed by the finance            本集團的財務部門由財務經理領導，
   manager is responsible for determining the policies and         負責確定金融工具公允價值計量的政
   procedures for the fair value measurement of financial          策和程序。財務部門直接向首席財務
   instruments. The finance department reports directly to         官匯報。財務部門於各報告日期分析
   the chief financial officer. At each reporting date, the        金融工具的價值變動，並確定估值中
   finance department analyses the movements in the values         應用的主要輸入數據。估值由首席財
   of financial instruments and determines the major inputs        務官審閱及批准。
   applied in the valuation. The valuation is reviewed and
   approved by the chief financial officer.


   The fair values of the financial assets and liabilities are     金融資產及負債的公允價值乃按該
   included at the amount at which the instrument could            工具於自願交易雙方當前交易（而非
   be exchanged in a current transaction between willing           強迫或清盤銷售）下的可交易金額入
   parties, other than in a forced or liquidation sale. The        賬。用於估計公允價值的方法及假設
   following methods and assumptions were used to estimate         如下：
   the fair values:


   The fair values of the non-current portion of financial         計入預付款項、按金及其他應收款項
   assets included in prepayments, deposits and other              的金融資產的非即期部分的公允價
   receivables, the non-current portion of other payables          值、其他應付款項及應計項目的非即
   and accruals and lease liabilities have been calculated by      期部分的公允價值以及租賃負債乃通

   currently available for instruments with similar terms,         餘期限的工具目前可用的利率，按預

   The fair value of a listed equity investment is based on        上市股權投資的公允價值乃基於市場

          parties. The main purpose of these financial instruments is           及應收關聯方款項。該等金融工具的
          to raise finance for the Group’s operations.                          主要目的是為本集團的營運籌集資金。

          The main risks arising from the Group’s financial                     本集團金融工具產生的主要風險為外

          Capital management                                              資本管理

          The primary objectives of the Group’s capital management        本集團資本管理的主要目標為保障本
          are to safeguard the Group’s ability to continue as a going     集團的持續經營能力及維持穩健的資
          concern and to maintain healthy capital ratios in order to      本比率，以支持其業務並將股東價值
          support its business and maximise shareholders’ value.          最大化。

          The Group manages its capital structure and makes               本集團根據經濟狀況變化及相關資產
          adjustments to it in light of changes in economic               的風險特徵來管理及調整其資本架
          conditions and the risk characteristics of the underlying       構。為維持或調整資本架構，本集團
          assets. To maintain or adjust the capital structure, the        可能會調整向股東派發的股息、向股
          Group may adjust the dividend payment to shareholders,          東返還資本或發行新股。
          return capital to shareholders or issue new shares.


          The Group monitors capital on the basis of the net gearing      本集團以淨負債率作為監控資本的基
          ratio. This ratio is calculated as total interest-bearing       準。淨負債率為按計息借款總額減去
          borrowings minus cash and cash equivalents divided by           現金及現金等價物除以截至報告期末
          total equity as of the end of the reporting period.             的權益總額計算。

          As at 31 December 2023 and 2022, the Group maintained           於2023年及2022 年12 月31 日，本集
          at net cash position.                                           團保持淨現金狀況。
